### 2026-08-27 — Anchor->organisation cascade extended to every remaining create flow; mobile forced-password-change; a wide design-consistency pass

**Anchor->organisation cascade, completed app-wide.** The "Super Admin must choose an anchor before the organisation field unlocks and narrows to it" pattern (already correct on Users/Organizations/Locations/Roles per the entries above) was still missing from Officers, Households, Vouchers, and Payroll's create/generate dialogs -- a Super Admin could pick an organisation there before ever choosing an anchor, from a list mixing every anchor's organisations together. Built a small shared composable, `useOrgCascade.ts` (`dialogAnchorId`, `dialogOrganizations`, `resetDialogScope()`), and wired it into: Register Field Officer; Add Household + its CSV bulk-upload dialog; Vouchers' Issue/Bulk-Issue/Generate-by-Area dialogs; and Payroll's generate-cycle wizard step 1. Each dialog now shows an `Anchor` select (Super Admin only, hidden entirely for an Anchor/Organisation Administrator, who never needed the step) with `Organisation` disabled until it's chosen and scoped to exactly that anchor once it is; field order in each dialog now puts Anchor/Organisation before the other details, matching the pattern already established on Organizations/Locations. `targetAnchorId` for every downstream `GET_HOUSEHOLDS`/`CREATE_*`/`BULK_*` call now comes from the dialog's own tracked anchor selection instead of a lookup back through the organisation list's `anchorId` field, which is simpler and doesn't depend on that list already carrying one. Verified live for both Super Admin (anchor selection correctly unlocks and scopes the organisation list to exactly that anchor's orgs, confirmed for all four dialogs) and Anchor Administrator (no anchor step shown, organisation list loads immediately scoped to their own anchor) -- plus the full backend round-trip via direct API calls for `CREATE_OFFICER`/`CREATE_HOUSEHOLD` under both roles.

Payments and Attendance were audited too but have no create dialog at all (payments come from payroll disbursement, attendance from the mobile app's clock-in/out) -- the only anchor+organisation picker on either page is the page-level view filter, which already re-scopes on anchor change; applied the same "Organisation disabled until Anchor is chosen" gate there for consistency, flagged to the user as a minor UX trade-off (loses the "jump straight to an org by name" shortcut on a page where picking the wrong one is harmless) since it's a judgment call rather than the same data-integrity concern a create flow has.

**Mobile forced-password-change**, closing the one piece explicitly flagged out-of-scope earlier in the project: the backend's `CHANGE_PASSWORD` handler already supported field officers (`actorRole=SUPERVISOR` -> `field_officers` table) and `LOGIN_SUPERVISOR` already returned `mustChangePassword` in its response -- no backend work needed. Added `SecurityActivity.newForcedIntent()` (a locked-down version of the existing Settings > Security > change-password screen: back navigation disabled via `OnBackPressedCallback`, its own copy, and on success proceeds straight into the same `SubscriptionGate` check `LoginActivity` would otherwise have run rather than returning to the settings screen) and wired `LoginActivity` to launch it instead of `HomeActivity` whenever `mustChangePassword` is true. Verified: both product flavors (`morphoSmart642`, `morphoSmart615`) compile clean, lint clean, unit tests pass, debug APK assembles -- not run on a physical device this pass.

**Design-consistency pass**, from a batch of screenshots showing real defects:
- **Root-caused a field/card border rendering bug**: some outlined fields (e.g. the Country field on New Organization) and bordered cards rendered with no visible border at all next to others with identical computed CSS (verified via direct DOM/computed-style comparison -- literally byte-for-byte identical `border-color`/`opacity`/class list) purely because Vuetify's 1px hairline border anti-aliases to near-invisible at some sub-pixel Y-positions under a fractional device-pixel ratio. Fixed by thickening every outlined-field and bordered-card border in a new global stylesheet (`src/styles/ui-fixes.css`, `border-width: 2px` / `1.5px` respectively) -- confirmed via zoomed screenshot comparison that this makes every previously-inconsistent field/card render identically crisp.
- **Disabled fields were nearly invisible**: Vuetify's default disabled-field opacity (0.38, the Material Design spec value) fades the *entire* outlined box -- border, background, and label together -- down to a ghost against a white dialog (e.g. the "Organisation" field waiting on an "Anchor" selection). Raised to 0.7 in the same stylesheet so a disabled field still reads as "a real field you can't edit yet."
- **Action-button columns wrapping vertically**: a data-table row's icon-only action buttons (view/edit/delete etc.) are plain inline `v-btn` siblings inside their `<td>`, so a squeezed column wrapped them onto separate lines instead of staying one row. Fixed with a `:has()`-based rule in the same stylesheet forcing every multi-button actions cell into a non-wrapping flex row, app-wide, rather than patching each page's markup individually.
- **Currency showing "US$0" instead of "USD 0"**: `Intl`'s `style: 'currency'` formatting renders USD as "US$" in some locales. Replaced every occurrence (Dashboard, Vouchers, Subscription) with a small shared `formatCurrency`/`formatCurrencyCompact` helper (`src/utils/currency.ts`) that always renders `"USD 1,234"` literally; Payroll already used this plain-prefix style and needed no change.
- **Dashboard "Amount generated by organisation" table**: its column headers were forced `text-align: center` by a blanket `.table-scroll :deep(th)` rule that overrode the numeric columns' own `.text-right` class, so headers sat centered above right-aligned data. Removed the blanket rule (no other page had it) -- headers and data now align exactly, verified live.
- **Dashboard metric cards**: the "Total Generated" card was hard-coded `wide: true` (spanning 2 grid columns, with a visibly larger icon), which -- combined with 9 total cards in a 4-column grid -- produced an uneven 4/4/1-with-one-oversized-card layout. Removed the `wide` special-case entirely (interface field, both card arrays, template class binding, and all associated CSS); every card is now the same size, confirmed live.
- **The two dashboard chart cards** ("Payment volume over time" / "Household registration trend") didn't match height, since neither `v-card` stretched to fill its `v-row`'s equal-height columns. Added `align-items: stretch` + `height: 100%` on the cards; confirmed live they now match exactly. Also removed the "Updated HH:MM" timestamp line under the dashboard greeting per the request, along with the now-unused `lastUpdated` ref.

Also: the roles list now sorts the current session's own scope first (`sortedRoles` computed, stable sort by scope-match), so a Super Admin sees "Super Admin" at the top of their own roles list instead of wherever it happened to fall in the unsorted order (previously third) -- and the page now auto-selects that same first-sorted role on load instead of whatever was first before sorting.

Also gave the Actions column header its title (`''` -> `'Actions'`, right-aligned) -- audited every other data table in the app for the same blank-header gap and found none.

Moved `Address` into the Organization-details column (5 fields) and `Household verification method` into the Authorized-contact column as its last field, using the select's own built-in `hint`/`persistent-hint` instead of a separate paragraph -- the two columns are now close to balanced (5 vs 4) and every field sits inside a real section.
- Frontend verified throughout: `vue-tsc -b --noEmit`, `npm run build`, and the `impeccable` design-lint detector all clean after every change; every fix above was additionally confirmed live in the browser (System Admin and Anchor/Organisation Administrator sessions via a directly-seeded JWT, never a typed password) before moving to the next one, not just code-reviewed.

### 2026-08-27 (continued) — The border-thickening fix from the entry above created a new visible seam; root-caused and fixed; household trend chart couldn't draw a line with one data point; re-verified the action-button and mobile-sync fixes

Flagged with fresh screenshots that fields now showed a *second* vertical line partway across the box (e.g. "Other names"), and that the household registration trend chart still wasn't showing a line, just a lone dot.

**Root cause of the new seam**: the border-width fix above (`.v-field__outline__start, __notch, __end { border-width: 2px }`) thickened *all four sides* of each of the three outline segments. By default those segments only carry a border on their outer-facing sides -- `__start`'s right edge and `__end`'s left edge butt seamlessly against `__notch` with no border between them, which is why a plain "make it thicker" rule had never shown a seam at 1px (sub-pixel rounding hid it) but did at 2px (confirmed directly via `getComputedStyle`: with the old rule active, `__start` had a real 2px solid border on *all* sides including the one facing `__notch`). Fixed by giving each segment only its own outer sides (`__start`: top/left/bottom; `__notch`: top only; `__end`: top/right/bottom) in `ui-fixes.css`. Verified live: the seam is gone on every field checked (Create dashboard user dialog, New Organization dialog's Country field -- the original invisible-border case) while the border is still fully visible everywhere.

**Household registration trend chart**: `LineChart.vue`'s smoothing path only draws a line between 2+ points; the dashboard's `DASHBOARD_HOUSEHOLDS_CHART`/`DASHBOARD_PAYMENTS_CHART` backend queries only return a bucket for months that actually have rows (plain `GROUP BY`, no date-spine), so an anchor with activity in a single calendar month got back exactly one point -- a dot with nothing to connect. Added a frontend `padMonthlySeries()` helper in `DashboardPage.vue` that pads both series out to a rolling 6-month window ending this month, zero-filling genuinely-empty months instead of omitting them; the "No X to chart yet" empty state now checks for any non-zero value in the padded series rather than just array length, so a truly all-zero window still shows the empty state instead of a flat line. Verified live: the household trend now renders a real curve across 2026-03..2026-08.

**Action buttons "still vertical"**: re-audited every list page with icon-only action buttons in a table cell (Households, Officers/Field Officers, Anchors, Organizations, Users, Vouchers, Locations) by checking each Actions cell's live computed `display`/`flex-direction`, not just eyeballing a screenshot -- every one is a non-wrapping horizontal flex row, matching the `:has()` fix from the entry above. Also checked outside `<td>` context (Household detail page's Alternates/Payment-history cards) for any icon-button group that might wrap the same way -- found none (those use text buttons with `prepend-icon` inside an already-flex card-title, or no per-row actions at all). Could not reproduce a remaining vertical-wrap instance; if one still exists it's in a spot not covered by this pass and needs a fresh screenshot to locate.

**Mobile <-> dashboard data flow**: traced the actual code path rather than assuming. Mobile's `UPLOAD_HOUSEHOLD_BIO` (`Biometric.java`) inserts directly into the same `households` table `Dashboard.householdsChart()` and `HouseholdsPage.vue` read from, with `created_at=GETDATE()` -- no separate mobile-only store, no queue the dashboard has to wait on; confirmed by reading the handler and by the live household-trend chart test above picking up real seeded rows immediately. The reverse direction (web changes reaching the mobile app) is intentionally not continuous: `SyncManager.syncAll()` only *pulls* the anchor's geography hierarchy and voucher catalogue on every sync pass (both fail open if offline, so registration is never blocked), while the payroll-cycle beneficiary roster is pulled specifically at `BIOMETRIC_LOGIN` time (per `PaymentDao`'s own header comment) rather than polled -- a deliberate offline-first design for a field disbursement round, not a gap. Nothing needed changing here; this was a verification pass, not a bug fix.
- Frontend verified: `vue-tsc --noEmit` clean. Every fix above confirmed live in the browser via a directly-seeded JWT (no typed password), not just code review. No test data left behind -- this pass was read-only browsing, no forms were submitted.

### 2026-08-27 (continued once more) — Checked progress.md for anything genuinely pending; the previous entry's seam fix was still broken on `v-select` fields, root-caused for real this time

**Progress.md pending-item check**: the only remaining unchecked `[ ]` items in the whole file are inside the frozen 2026-08-22 checklist (sections K through Q, lines ~700-733) -- that block is deliberately never edited in place (its own header says so: "do not rewrite this whole block -- append dated continuation notes below it"). Cross-checked every one of K/L/M/N/O/Q against later entries in this file and confirmed each was completed and separately marked `[x]` there (line 750 K, 752 L, 753 M, 754 N, 757 Q; O/System-Owner-role covered extensively across many entries from 2026-08-25 onward). Section P's original ask (remove the mobile Profile tab) was superseded by an explicit later decision to keep it as a real three-tab Home/Profile/Settings nav, matching the user's own reference design -- not an oversight. Nothing is actually pending.

**The border fix from the entry above was incomplete.** Flagged with a fresh screenshot of a `Status` filter select showing a visibly uneven/"broken" top border. Root cause: `.v-field__outline__notch`'s own `border-width` rule was dead code -- per the CSS spec, an element with `border-style: none` (Vuetify's default for that element) always computes its border-width to `0` no matter what width is declared, confirmed directly via `getComputedStyle` (`"0px none"` on the notch itself, even with `border-width: 2px 0 0 0 !important` targeting it). The notch's *actual* visible top border is drawn by its `::before` (and, once a label floats, `::after`) pseudo-elements, which were still sitting at Vuetify's original ~0.667px hairline -- so every field showed a thick 2px cap (`__start`), a visibly thinner ~0.667px middle strip (`__notch`), then a thick 2px cap again (`__end`): a real thickness mismatch, not a rendering artifact. Fixed by moving the rule onto `.v-field__outline__notch::before`/`::after` directly (`border-top-width: 2px`) instead of the notch element itself. Verified via `getComputedStyle` that all four segments (`start`, `notch::before`, `notch::after`, `end`) now report an identical `2px solid` top border, and via zoomed screenshot on both a `v-select` (Households page's "Status" filter) and a text field (`Registered from`/`Registered to`) that the border now reads as one continuous, uniform line with no seam and no thickness change anywhere.
- Frontend verified: `vue-tsc --noEmit` clean. Confirmed live via a directly-seeded JWT, not just computed-style inspection.

### 2026-08-27 (continued once more, again) — Verified the whole 2026-08-26/27 anchor/RBAC/mobile request against actual code (not the log's own claims), then found and fixed the real remaining border bug the previous two passes both missed

Asked to go through progress.md item by item and confirm everything the file claims is actually true in the codebase. Spot-checked every discrete claim in the 2026-08-26/27 entries above against the real files rather than re-reading the log: anchor-gate removal and filter repositioning (all six list pages plus Locations' four tabs plus Organizations/Roles), Anchors page CRUD, organisation-code removal, Super Admin deactivation floor, Roles & Permissions locks removed, `useOrgCascade.ts` wiring, `AccessDeniedPage.vue` role-aware copy, mobile nav de-duplication and forced-password-change, and the `ui-fixes.css` fixes — all genuinely present and matching their descriptions. One cosmetic log/code drift found and not worth fixing: `Administration.java`'s `saveRole` now lets a Super Admin both create and edit SYSTEM-scope roles, not edit-only as an earlier entry above describes; still safely gated on `isSystemAdmin`, just a stale sentence.

**Then a real bug, flagged live with a screenshot of the Users page's "Search users" field showing an uneven/faint bottom border.** Root-caused for real this time by reading Vuetify's actual `VField.css` source instead of guessing further: `__notch::before` is genuinely the field's top border (Vuetify: `border-width: var(--v-field-border-width) 0 0`), but `__notch::after` is genuinely the **bottom** border (`bottom: 0; border-width: 0 0 var(--v-field-border-width)`) — the entry above's fix set `border-top-width` on *both*, so `::before`'s top border landed correctly at 2px, but `::after`'s real border (its bottom one) was left at the un-overridden default (1px unfocused / 2px focused, 0.38 opacity default), while `__start`/`__end`'s bottom borders were separately hardcoded to 2px — producing a bottom edge that's thick at the two 12px end-caps and thin across the entire middle (nearly the whole field, since the notch has no floating label to shrink it when the field is empty and unfocused). The two prior sessions' `getComputedStyle` checks both only ever inspected the *top* border, which is why this survived two "verified" passes.

**Replaced all of the hand-rolled per-segment/per-side border-width rules with one line**: `.v-field--variant-outlined .v-field__outline { --v-field-border-width: 2px; }`. Every segment (`__start`, `__notch::before`, `__notch::after`, `__end`) already reads its border-width from this single CSS variable in Vuetify's own source, on every side that segment actually borders — overriding the variable once means Vuetify's own (already-correct, seam-free-by-construction) per-side logic just works, instead of re-deriving it by hand and getting one side wrong. Verified via `getComputedStyle` on the live Users-page search field: all four segments now report `2px` on every side they draw (`start`: top/bottom/left; `notchBefore`: top; `notchAfter`: bottom; `end`: top/bottom/right), identical opacity and color throughout, in both the unfocused/inactive state and the focused/notched-label state. Re-checked the original "invisible Country field" case (New Organization dialog) live — clean, uniform, visible border on all four sides.

**Second real bug, same screenshot's implicit complaint about card borders**: `style.css`'s blanket `.v-card` rule paired a real border with `box-shadow: 0 10px 28px -20px rgba(15,118,110,.35)` — a downward-offset shadow that only casts below/beside a card, never above it, so any bordered card read as having a heavier/blurrier edge on some sides than others regardless of how uniform the border-width itself was. Removed the box-shadow entirely from that rule (cards already communicate their boundary via the border; a directional shadow on top of that can't ever be uniform by construction). Verified live on the Users page's `admin-card`: `getComputedStyle` confirms `boxShadow: "none"`, and the top/side edges now render as one crisp, identical-weight line with no shadow bleed.
- Frontend verified: `vue-tsc -b --noEmit` clean (CSS-only change, no `.vue`/`.ts` files touched). Confirmed live via a directly-seeded JWT (Super Admin), not just computed-style inspection — Users page search field and role filter (both states), and the New Organization dialog's Country field.

### 2026-08-27 (continued once more, again, again) — "Actions" column header was right-aligned while its icon buttons render left-aligned

Flagged with a screenshot: every data table's "Actions" header sat flush right while the row's pencil/toggle/trash (or pencil/text-button) icons sat flush left beneath it -- a real mismatch, not a matter of taste. Root cause: the header definition passed `align: 'end'` (Vuetify then right-aligns both the `<th>` and, normally, the `<td>`), but the `:has(> .v-btn + .v-btn)` rule added in an earlier pass (see the border-fix entry above) forces those action cells into `display: flex` with no `justify-content` set -- flexbox ignores `text-align`, so the buttons default to flex-start (left) regardless of the column's declared alignment, while the header text -- not a flex child -- still honored `align: 'end'` and stayed right.

Changed `align: 'end'` to `align: 'start'` on the "Actions" column header in all 10 places it appears (`AnchorsPage.vue`, `OfficersPage.vue`, `LocationsPage.vue`, `UsersPage.vue`, `PaymentsPage.vue`, `HouseholdsPage.vue`, `PayrollPage.vue`, `VouchersPage.vue`, `SubscriptionPage.vue`, `OrganizationsPage.vue`) -- matching where the buttons already render rather than changing the flex rule to chase the header the other way. Confirmed no other table header in the app uses `align: 'end'`/`'right'`; the numeric-column right-alignment mentioned in an earlier entry (Dashboard's org-amount table) uses a separate plain-`<v-table>` `.text-right` CSS class, not this `headers` array mechanism, and was intentionally left as-is since there the data itself is also right-aligned (no mismatch).
- Frontend verified: `vue-tsc -b --noEmit` clean. Confirmed live via a directly-seeded JWT on both Users (icon + text-button Actions cell) and Anchors (icon-only Actions cell) -- header now sits directly above the buttons on both.

Selecting a date immediately reloads only that card; changing its grouping reinterprets the same selected date as the corresponding day, Monday–Sunday week, calendar month, or calendar year. Request sequencing prevents a slower previous selection from overwriting the latest chart.

### 2026-09-01 — Landing-page image refresh + the real root cause of the nav scrollspy bug (the 2026-08-28 IntersectionObserver fix was gone/replaced and broken)

- **Swapped landing-page photography.** User-supplied PNGs from `Downloads/landing page` (`cash transfer`, `biometric verification`, `deduplication`, `voucher redemption`) replaced the four matching hero-carousel backgrounds in `frontend/public/hero/`; `Downloads/how it works images` (`sync when signal returns`, `verify at disbursment`) replaced two of the four how-it-works carousel photos in `frontend/public/how-it-works/`. `ai-agent.webp`, `register-in-the-field.webp`, and `reconcile-on-the-dashboard.webp` had no supplied replacement and were left as-is. Converted PNG→WEBP with `sharp` (`npm install --no-save sharp` in `frontend/`, used only as a one-off local conversion tool, then removed — `package.json`/lockfile untouched) so page weight stayed in the same ~100-220KB range instead of shipping the original 2-2.5MB PNGs directly. No template/script changes were needed since the new files reuse the exact existing filenames/paths.
- **Root-caused the nav-highlight bug for real.** User report: clicking Mission/How it works/Platform/Product in `LandingPage.vue` scrolled the page but the *previous* link stayed highlighted orange. The current code (not what the 2026-08-28 entry above describes — that IntersectionObserver approach is gone, replaced at some point with a manual `getBoundingClientRect` + scroll-event/rAF scrollspy) had two independent, uncoordinated offsets: `:global(html) { scroll-padding-top: 6rem }` *and* `.landing-root section, footer { scroll-margin-top: 6rem }` stack additively per the CSS scroll-into-view spec, landing a clicked section's top at ~192px from the viewport top — while the JS scrollspy's activation line was only `nav.offsetHeight + 16` (~68px). The section never crossed the line the spy was watching, so the previously-active link never lost its highlight.
- **Fix in `frontend/src/pages/LandingPage.vue`:** removed the redundant `scroll-margin-top` block entirely; extracted a single `navActivationLine()` helper (`nav.offsetHeight + 16`) shared by both the scrollspy check and a new `syncNavScrollOffset()` that sets `document.documentElement.style.scrollPaddingTop` from that same measurement at mount and on resize, so the native anchor-scroll target and the JS activation line can never drift apart again. Also added a 2px tolerance on the spy's comparison (a live-measured case showed the scroll settling 0.17px over the exact line and missing its own check) and a `scrollend` listener as a safety net for the case where a smooth anchor-jump settles without a final `scroll` event at its exact rest position.
- **Verified live** against the already-running local dev server (`localhost:5175`) with real trusted mouse clicks via `claude-in-chrome`, from fresh page loads: Mission → How it works → Platform → Product each scrolled to and correctly highlighted only its own nav link, checked both visually and via `document.querySelector('.nav-links a.active')`. Note for next time: synthetic `element.click()` calls (no real user gesture) do **not** trigger the browser's native anchor-scroll-into-view behavior in this environment and will falsely look like a regression — always drive nav-link tests through real coordinate/ref clicks, not JS-dispatched clicks. `npx vue-tsc --noEmit` passes clean.

### 2026-09-01 — Permission-gated "Pay Online" payment recovery, end to end from mobile failure to dashboard action

- Added a failed-payment recovery flow: when a generated payment fails field biometric verification, it now moves to a new FAILED status (2) instead of sitting PENDING forever, and the System Owner can recover it from the Payments page with a **Pay Online** action gated by a new `PAY_ONLINE` permission. That permission is deliberately *not* auto-granted to any built-in role (unlike the other dashboard-area permissions in migration 025) — the System Owner has it automatically because that role bypasses permission checks entirely (`TenantScope.isSystemOwner`), and every other role only sees the button once the System Owner explicitly assigns `PAY_ONLINE` to it from the Roles page. This mirrors the existing `ACCESS_*` permission-and-role-assignment pattern rather than inventing a new mechanism.
- **Database:** added `database/migrations/038_payment_online_recovery.sql` — `payment_channel`/`online_reference` columns on `payments`, backfilled `CASH` for already-paid rows, and seeds the `PAY_ONLINE` permission (system-defined, no role grant). Must be applied via `database/run-migrations.ps1` before this goes live on a deployed database.
- **Backend:** `PermissionPolicy.java` maps a new `PAY_PAYMENT_ONLINE` action to `PAY_ONLINE`. `Payment.java` gets a `PAY_PAYMENT_ONLINE` handler that only accepts a currently-FAILED payment, flips it to paid with `payment_channel='ONLINE'` and a generated reference, and `PAYMENT_SUMMARY` now also returns `failedCount`/`paidAmount`. `Biometric.java`'s `RECORD_FIELD_PAYMENT` now branches on `biometricVerified` for a generated payment: false moves it to FAILED (status=2) instead of being rejected outright, which is the actual mechanism that gets a payment into the recoverable state — previously a failed field verification was never communicated to the backend at all.
- **Dashboard:** `PaymentsPage.vue` gained a Failed status filter/chip, a fourth "Failed" summary card, a "Mark as failed" action (existing `ACCESS_PAYMENTS` permission) to reach that state, and the permission-gated "Pay Online" button (`auth.can('PAY_ONLINE')`) that only shows for a FAILED row. `permissionCatalog.ts` registers `PAY_ONLINE` in the Households & alternates group so it's assignable from the Roles page; `processingCodes.ts` types the new `PAY_PAYMENT_ONLINE` code.
- **Mobile:** `PaymentDao.java` adds `STATUS_FAILED` and `markGeneratedPaymentFailed()`. `PaymentVerificationActivity.java`'s `showFailure()` now actually persists the failure locally and triggers a sync for a generated payment (previously it only navigated to a local failure screen and left the remote payment PENDING with no record anything went wrong). `SyncManager.java` uploads `biometricVerified=false` for a row marked FAILED instead of always claiming success. `PaymentListAdapter.java` plus a new `bg_status_error` drawable/`payment_failed` string show "Failed" distinctly in the on-device payment history under More, instead of a failed row being mislabelled Pending.

### 2026-09-01 — Mobile 16 KB page-size compliance (Play Console rejection fix)

- Play Console/APK Analyzer flagged `app-morphoSmart615-debug.apk` as non-compliant with 16 KB devices: `libimage_processing_util_jni.so` (CameraX), `libonnxruntime.so`, and `libonnxruntime4j_jni.so` (ONNX Runtime) had LOAD segments aligned to 4 KB, not 16 KB — required for all Play submissions targeting Android 15+ since 2025-11-01. `useLegacyPackaging=true` (already set) does **not** fix this: that only avoids the zip-entry-alignment complaint, not the ELF program-header alignment the Play Console check actually reads, which is a property baked into the compiled `.so` itself.
- **Verified with ground truth, not release notes:** release notes/GitHub issues for both libraries were vague/contradictory about which version actually fixed alignment, so wrote a small Python ELF program-header parser (`check_align.py`, scratchpad — not committed) and directly inspected `.so` files pulled from candidate AAR versions. Confirmed: CameraX `camera-core` fixed at 1.4.0 (bumped to 1.4.2, latest 1.4.x patch); ONNX Runtime's `libonnxruntime.so` fixed at 1.21.0 but `libonnxruntime4j_jni.so` (the JNI wrapper) not until 1.22.0 — needed both, so 1.22.0 is the true minimum. Went with 1.29.0 (latest at the time) since the API surface this app touches (`OrtEnvironment`/`OrtSession`/`OnnxTensor`, see `OnnxFaceEmbedder.java`) is stable and unchanged.
- **Blocker discovered along the way, resolved by explicit user decision:** onnxruntime-android 1.22.0+ requires `minSdk 24`. A previous session (2026-08-xx, see the `onnxruntime-android` pin entry above) deliberately stayed on 1.18.0 specifically to preserve this app's `minSdk 21` (Android 5.0/5.1/6.0 support). Asked the user directly rather than silently overriding that prior tradeoff; they chose to raise `minSdk` to 24, so `app/build.gradle.kts`'s `minSdk` is now 24. `tools:overrideLibrary` (Google's own escape hatch) was considered and rejected — not appropriate for a biometric payment app given Google's own warning that it "may lead to runtime failures."
- **Changed:** `gradle/libs.versions.toml` — `camerax` 1.3.4 → 1.4.2, `onnxruntimeAndroid` 1.18.0 → 1.29.0. `app/build.gradle.kts` — `minSdk` 21 → 24.
- **Verification (re-run independently):** extracted the actual built `app-morphoSmart615-debug.apk`/`app-morphoSmart642-debug.apk` and re-ran the ELF alignment check against every `arm64-v8a/*.so` inside — all now report 16384+ byte LOAD alignment (the MorphoSmart vendor `.so`s were already 65536-aligned, untouched). `assembleMorphoSmart615Debug`/`assembleMorphoSmart642Debug` — BUILD SUCCESSFUL. `lintMorphoSmart615Debug`/`lintMorphoSmart642Debug` — the only lint *error* is a pre-existing, unrelated `local.properties` Windows-path-escaping issue (lint auto-fixed it in place); the minSdk bump did surface 4 new `ObsoleteSdkInt` warnings in `FaceEmbeddingCipher.java` (its API-23 plaintext-fallback guards are now unreachable dead branches since minSdk 24 > 23) — cleaned those up (`isSupported()` now just returns `true`, removed the dead `SDK_INT` checks and the now-redundant `@RequiresApi(M)`/`Build` import), re-ran lint clean (104 warnings, same 1 pre-existing unrelated error, 0 new). `testMorphoSmart615/642DebugUnitTest` — BUILD SUCCESSFUL, both flavors. **Did not** touch `FaceDao`'s plaintext-fallback storage path itself (schema/`embedding_encrypted` column, the broader "what happens on unsupported devices" design) — that's now permanently dead code given minSdk 24 guarantees Keystore AES is always available, but removing it is a separate, security-relevant design decision left for the user to make deliberately rather than as a side effect of this fix.
- **Not touched:** `com.google.mlkit:face-detection` (`libface_detector_v2_jni.so`) — the Play Console warning didn't list it, and the built APK confirms it's already 16 KB-aligned at the current pinned version (16.1.7); left alone to keep the diff minimal.

- **Migrations:** confirmed a completed `run-migrations.ps1` run applied all scripts 000 → 038 cleanly (exit code 0). Independently verified on the live DB (not just trusted from the log, and only after the user explicitly approved the one-off `sqlcmd` query — direct queries against the live 173.249.55.90 DB are gated by Claude Code's auto-mode classifier): 027's canonical-hierarchy repair left exactly one role per canonical name (`Super Admin`/SYSTEM, `Anchor Administrator`/ANCHOR, `Organisation Administrator`/ORGANISATION, no duplicates, no leftover unrenamed `System Owner`) and zero users with a mismatched ANCHOR/ORGANISATION admin role; 034's multi-admin support is confirmed not just structurally (the old `UX_users_single_system_owner` unique index is gone) but in active use — two live `is_system_admin=1` accounts already coexist. - **Real-device login fix:** an Idemia/MorphoSmart tablet connecting over Wi-Fi couldn't reach the backend — the app was built with `backend/.env`'s `BIOPAY_MOBILE_API_BASE_URL=http://10.0.2.2:7730/biopay`, and `10.0.2.2` is the Android **emulator's** host-loopback alias, meaningless to a real device. Diagnosed the backend was in fact already listening on `0.0.0.0:7730` (confirmed via `netstat`), so the only real gaps were (a) no Windows Firewall inbound rule for port 7730 — user added one manually via an elevated PowerShell (`netsh advfirewall firewall add rule name="BioPay backend 7730" dir=in action=allow protocol=TCP localport=7730`, confirmed working) — and (b) the baked-in base URL. Changed `backend/.env`'s `BIOPAY_MOBILE_API_BASE_URL` to the dev machine's LAN IP (`http://192.168.100.163:7730/biopay`) as the new build default and rebuilt `assembleMorphoSmart615Debug`. **Not committed** — `.env` is gitignored, this is a local/machine-specific value. **Tradeoff worth knowing:** future Android-emulator builds on this machine now need `-PbiopayApiBaseUrl=http://10.0.2.2:7730/biopay` passed explicitly, since the emulator-friendly default was replaced.
- **Login screen logo:** replaced the icon shown in `activity_login.xml`'s brand panel with the new fingerprint+badge mark cropped out of a fuller `biopay_logo_main.png` logo lockup the user supplied (which also contained a "BioPay" wordmark and tagline baked into the same flattened image — used only the icon portion, since the layout already renders `app_name`/`login_brand_supporting` as separate text next to it, and pasting the full flattened lockup would have duplicated that text and shown its opaque white background against the layout's green panel). Cropped, background-removed (flood-fill from the crop border, not global white-keying, so the icon's internal white fingerprint ridges survive), and cleaned up one small disconnected artifact speck from the source image. **Saved as a new file, `biopay_logo_mark_login.png`, not overwritten in place over `biopay_logo_mark.png`** — that original filename is also the app's launcher icon (`AndroidManifest.xml` `android:icon`/`android:roundIcon`) and is used on the Home and Splash screens too; the ask was login-page-only, so only `activity_login.xml`'s `ImageView` was repointed. (First pass did edit the shared file in place before catching the launcher-icon collision — reverted via `git checkout` before it got committed.) `assembleMorphoSmart615Debug` — BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
- Verification: backend `mvn test` passes all tests; frontend `vue-tsc -b --noEmit` and `npm run build` are clean (this also fixed the pre-existing `PaymentsPage.vue`/`ProcessingCode` build break noted in the entry above, which was this work's `PAY_PAYMENT_ONLINE` reference landing in `PaymentsPage.vue` before `processingCodes.ts` was updated); an Android debug variant (`compileMorphoSmart642DebugJavaWithJavac`) compiles clean with only pre-existing Java-8-target deprecation warnings. Not verified in a live browser or on-device session this pass — that, and applying migration 038 to a real database, remain open for next time.

### 2026-09-01 — Ported IDEMIA Peripheral Management AIDL (fixes "scanner not connected" on the ID Screen tablet)

- **Root cause of the real-device symptom** (physically connected fingerprint sensor, Settings still shows "not connected", capture fails): on ID Screen / ID Screen 60 tablets the fingerprint sensor's power rail and host USB port default **off** to save battery, per IDEMIA's own guide. `MorphoDeviceAdapter`'s `isAvailable()`/`open()` were only ever enumerating USB devices — with the sensor unpowered, the SDK's USB scan legitimately finds nothing, so this was never a cable/build/permission problem, it was an un-powered port. This is exactly the gap the 642 adapter's javadoc already flagged as "not yet ported... will be added... once the core capture/verify path is verified end-to-end" and the 2026-08-23 entry documented in full but left un-ported on explicit user hold-off. User hit the real blocker on hardware this session and asked to port it.
- **Source of the interface, and an honest caveat about it:** the user re-supplied `Programming_Guide_260822_161533.pdf` (previously reviewed 2026-08-23, not saved to disk after that session — had to be re-requested). Extracted its Peripheral Management chapter via `pdftotext`. The guide documents `PeripheralsPowerInterface`'s method usage (`setFingerPrintSwitch`/`getFingerPrintSwitch`, `setHostUsbPortSwitch`/`getHostUsbPortSwitch`, `setDockingStationUsbPortSwitch`/`getDockingStationUsbPortSwitch`, `setNfcSwitch`/`getNfcSwitch`, `setUSBRole`/`getUSBRole`) but does **not** ship the raw `.aidl` source — confirmed by searching this machine (Downloads, the `morpho` reference folders, `.gradle` caches) for an existing `PeripheralsPowerInterface.aidl` or any bundled ID Screen sample project; none exists. The new `mobile/agent/app/src/morphoSmart642/aidl/com/idemia/peripherals/PeripheralsPowerInterface.aidl` was therefore authored from the guide's documented method list, in the guide's own presentation order. **This is a real, flagged risk, not a solved one:** AIDL binder transaction IDs are assigned by declaration order, so if IDEMIA's actual service interface orders these methods differently, a call could silently invoke the wrong remote method instead of failing loudly. Documented directly in the `.aidl` file's header comment; recommend a set/get round-trip check (`setFingerPrintSwitch(true)` then `getFingerPrintSwitch()` reads back `true`) on real hardware before trusting this beyond the two switches `MorphoDeviceAdapter` actually calls.
- **Implementation, `morphoSmart642` flavor only** (the flavor progress.md's 2026-08-23 entry confirmed matches this guide's SDK 6.42.0.0 — not `morphoSmart615`): `app/build.gradle.kts` sets `buildFeatures.aidl = true`. `MorphoDeviceAdapter.java` gained `powerOnFingerprintPeripherals(Activity)` — binds `idemia.intent.action.CONN_PERIPHERALS_SERVICE_AIDL` against package `com.android.settings` with a 2s `CountDownLatch` wait, flips `setHostUsbPortSwitch(true)` + `setFingerPrintSwitch(true)`, then unbinds (the switches are system-level hardware state, not tied to the binding's lifetime — no need to hold the service bound across a whole capture session). Called at the top of both `isAvailable()` and `open()`, before any USB enumeration. Best-effort by design: binder/service errors are caught and logged, never thrown, so behavior on hardware without this service (emulator, non-ID-Screen device) is unchanged from before this fix.
- **Not implemented / deliberately out of scope:** USB role switching (`setUSBRole`/`getUSBRole`) — the guide's Auto mode should already grant peripheral access whenever no PC is tethered, which is the normal field-agent case, and forcing Host mode has a real side effect (disables PC/ADB connection over that port) not worth taking on without a reported need. Docking-station USB and NFC switches — unused by this app's fingerprint-only flow. All three are already in the `.aidl` surface if a future need shows up.
- **Verification:** `compileMorphoSmart642DebugJavaWithJavac` (via `compileMorphoSmart642DebugAidl` first) — BUILD SUCCESSFUL, confirms the AIDL-generated `PeripheralsPowerInterface.Stub` compiles against the new adapter code. `compileMorphoSmart615DebugJavaWithJavac` — BUILD SUCCESSFUL, confirms the untouched flavor is unaffected (the new `.aidl`/import only exist under `src/morphoSmart642/`). **Not yet verified on real ID Screen hardware** — that's the one check that actually matters here (does the sensor now enumerate, and does the set/get round-trip above confirm the method ordering is right) and remains open for the user's next on-device session.

### 2026-09-02 — Dashboard chart correctness: axis-overshoot bug, a real payments-status filter bug, and two chart-form iterations

- **Reported symptom:** the Registrations card's line chart dipped visibly below the x-axis after a spike, and showed "0 households" despite the system having households. **Root cause of the dip:** `LineChart.vue`'s Catmull-Rom smoothing spline extrapolates control points past sharp peaks/valleys — a single-point spike (0→1→0, common for these low-integer daily counts) overshoots past both axes with nothing in the data backing it. **The "0 households" was not a bug:** that card sums new registrations within the selected period ("month" by default); the system's existing households were registered in earlier months, so 0 for the current month-to-date was correct. Confirmed by checking `Household.java`'s insert paths (both single and CSV-bulk) — `created_at` is always set via `GETDATE()`, `status` is always `1`, no NULL/orphan-join explanation existed.
- **Second bug found while investigating, not initially reported — a real data-correctness issue:** `payments.status` is 0=pending / 1=paid / 2=failed (confirmed in `Payment.java`), but several `Dashboard.java` queries never filtered by it, so pending/failed payments were silently counted as completed activity. Fixed by adding `status=1` (or `f.status=1` for fingerprints) to: `anchorMetrics.paymentsAgg` (the anchor "value disbursed"/"payments completed" totals), the Payment Volume chart's cash series (`paymentsChart`'s `cashSql`, both anchor and org scope), `amountsByOrganisation`'s cash subquery ("Amount generated by organisation" table), and `registeredFingerprints` in both `anchorMetrics` and `organisationMetrics` (was counting every fingerprint row including superseded/inactive ones — every other fingerprint query in the codebase already filters `status=1`, this was the one inconsistent spot). Vouchers and household/alternate counts were already correctly filtered and untouched.
- **Chart-form iteration 1 — stacked bars:** per the `dataviz` skill's form-selection guidance (sparse discrete counts shouldn't be smoothed/interpolated; two additive series read better stacked), rebuilt both dashboard charts as a new `BarChart.vue` (stacked, 2px inter-segment gap, rounded top corners, per-bar hover tooltip). While building a preview harness to eyeball it, caught a **second, more fundamental axis bug that also explains the original dip**: the `niceStep`/`niceMax` axis-scaling helper (shared unchanged from the old `LineChart.vue`) rounded to the *nearest* clean tick step instead of *up*, so the computed axis max could end up smaller than the actual data max — a single spike could poke past the top of the plot regardless of chart type. Fixed the rounding thresholds (`<=` ceiling instead of nearest-neighbor) and added a hard clamp as a safety net so a mark can never render outside the plot bounds.
- **Chart-form iteration 2 — back to lines, on user direction:** user asked to revert Registrations to a line chart for reading pace (rising/falling), and name the card "Registration trend"; then asked to also switch Payment volume to lines for direct cash-vs-voucher comparison (a stacked bar only anchors the bottom segment to the shared zero baseline, making the top segment's magnitude harder to compare against it). Rewrote `LineChart.vue` with the same fixed axis-scaling logic but **plain straight polylines instead of a Catmull-Rom spline** — the actual fix for the original bug report, since a straight segment between two points can't overshoot past the range of those two points. `BarChart.vue` (the dashboard-specific one) is consequently unused by the dashboard now.
- **Mistake caught and fixed before it shipped:** creating the dashboard's `BarChart.vue` overwrote a *different*, pre-existing `frontend/src/components/BarChart.vue` (single-series, used by `HouseholdsPage.vue`'s "By status" breakdown chart, different prop shape entirely — `xAxisLabel`/`yAxisLabel`/`chartHeight`/`showValues`) instead of using a distinct filename. Caught via `git status` showing it as modified rather than untracked. Restored via `git checkout` and confirmed `HouseholdsPage.vue`'s chart still renders correctly.
- **Verification:** `npx vue-tsc -b` clean after every edit. Live-verified in-browser (Claude-in-Chrome), logged in as `anchor.admin@frontiertrust.bank` on the already-running local backend (`OTP_REQUIRED=true` live on this account — user completed the email/authenticator step manually mid-session). Confirmed on the real dashboard: Registration trend and Payment volume both render as straight lines with no axis overshoot even on all-zero data; hover tooltips read correct per-day values (checked via direct DOM dispatch, e.g. day 2 → "Households: 2 / Alternates: 1"); Payments Completed / Amount-generated-by-organisation cash totals now read `USD 0` consistently with each other post-fix; Households page's unrelated bar chart unaffected; no console errors. Backend jar on port 7730 already reflected the `Dashboard.java` fix by the time it was checked (rebuilt by something in the environment between the edit and the check — not rebuilt manually via `mvn package` this session).

User wanted the tab to land on an all-payments list (like the one already living under More → Payments) with a **Pay** button at the top leading into that existing flow, and the rest of the payments below it.
- **Made `PaymentsActivity` (the filterable All/Paid/Pending payment register, previously only reachable via More) the tab-root landing screen for `navPayment`:** `BaseActivity.java`'s `setupMainNavigation` now routes `navPayment` to `PaymentsActivity.class` instead of `GeneratePaymentActivity.class`. `PaymentsActivity.java` now calls `setupMainNavigation`/`setupPaymentFab` (was `setupBackToolbar`) and wires a new `btnPay` button to launch `GeneratePaymentActivity`. `activity_payments.xml` gained that primary `Pay` button above the existing filter row and the shared `partial_bottom_nav_bar` include; its back-arrow toolbar icon was dropped since it's now a tab root, not a sub-screen.
- **Demoted `GeneratePaymentActivity` to a sub-screen** reached from that Pay button (and still from the global FAB / `PaymentResultActivity`'s "Another payment" shortcut, both unchanged): swapped its `setupMainNavigation`/`setupPaymentFab`/bottom-nav-include for `setupBackToolbar` plus a back arrow on its toolbar, matching the existing pattern used by `PaymentVerificationActivity`/`VoucherRedemptionActivity`. Its toolbar `android:title` alone didn't render a visible heading in practice, so also added the same "big `BioPay.ScreenTitle` text below the toolbar" pattern those other sub-screens use, showing "Generated payment" as an actual on-screen header (follow-up fix after the user spotted the missing header live on-device).
- **Nav-indicator cleanup:** with `PaymentsActivity` now genuinely selected as the active tab, Material's default active-indicator pill (`colorSecondaryContainer`, `#FFE2C3` peach) rendered behind the elevated Payment FAB as an visible halo around it — cosmetically fine behind a flat icon (Home/Households/Activity/More) but bad behind a raised circular button. `BottomNavigationView` only exposes one `itemActiveIndicatorStyle` for the whole bar, not per-item, so there's no way to keep the pill on the other four tabs while dropping it only for Payment without a custom item view; went with removing it bar-wide via a new `BioPay.NavigationActiveIndicator` style (`styles.xml`, extends `Widget.Material3.BottomNavigationView.ActiveIndicator` with `android:color` transparent) applied through `partial_bottom_nav_bar.xml`'s `app:itemActiveIndicatorStyle`. Live-verified end to end on the connected real device (`MPH-MB003A`, serial `5041628786`, `morphoSmart642` debug build reinstalled after each change): Payment tab now opens the list ("0 payment(s) shown", All/Paid/Pending filters, no orange halo behind the FAB); tapping **Pay** opens `GeneratePaymentActivity` showing the "Generated payment" header and a working back arrow back to the list; Home tab's own active-indicator pill confirmed gone too (expected, bar-wide change) with its green icon/label tint still showing it's selected. Did not run the mobile unit test suite this session (no test changes; scope was layout/navigation only).

### 2026-09-02 — Payment nav-tap fix (the FAB was still hijacking taps), nav bar shrunk, Home hero decluttered

- **Removed the now-redundant "Payments" row from More:** the previous entry made `navPayment` land on `PaymentsActivity` directly, so `More`'s own `rowPayments` (→ the same `PaymentsActivity`) was a duplicate entry point. Dropped it from `MoreActivity.java` and `activity_more.xml`, plus the now-unused `more_payments`/`more_payments_body` strings.

Diagnosed live via `adb`/screencaps — the elevated circular `fabPayment` FAB (left over from the old center "Scan" action, repurposed to jump straight into `GeneratePaymentActivity`) visually sat on top of the plain `navPayment` item (which used an invisible `ic_nav_spacer` icon specifically so the FAB could occupy that slot) and physically intercepted the tap. A tap anywhere near the icon hit the FAB, not the nav item underneath.
- **Fix: removed the FAB entirely** rather than repoint it, since the user's original ask was one single Payment entry point. Simplified `partial_bottom_nav_bar.xml` from a `CoordinatorLayout` + `BottomNavigationView` + `FloatingActionButton` down to a plain `BottomNavigationView` (needed its own `xmlns:android`/`xmlns:app` once it became the root element). Deleted `setupPaymentFab()` from `BaseActivity.java` and its call site in all five screens that had it (`HomeActivity`, `HouseholdListActivity`, `ActivityFeedActivity`, `MoreActivity`, `PaymentsActivity`). Gave `navPayment` back a real icon (`ic_payments`, same as everywhere else it's used) instead of the spacer, and deleted the now-unused `ic_nav_spacer.xml` drawable and `payment_nav_action` string.
- **Stale build-output trap found and fixed:** `mobile/agent/build.gradle.kts` redirects `layout.buildDirectory` for every module to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\BioPay\gradle-build\agent\...` (likely to dodge OneDrive locking the build folder, since the repo lives under `OneDrive\Desktop\bio pay\biopay`). A leftover, gitignored `mobile/agent/app/build` from before that redirect (or from another tool) was still sitting in the repo with old compiled output; installing from it produced a real crash unrelated to this session's change (`SQLiteException: Can't downgrade database from version 13 to 10`, from a stale cached `DatabaseHelper`). Deleted the stale `mobile/agent/app/build` and `mobile/agent/build` directories (both gitignored, both regenerate automatically); going forward the real APK is always under the `%LOCALAPPDATA%\BioPay\gradle-build\agent\app\outputs\apk\...` path.
- **Nav bar sized down twice this session** in response to live feedback: first `main_navigation_height` 76dp→64dp (80dp→68dp on `sw600dp`) once the FAB's headroom was no longer needed, then 64dp→56dp (68dp→60dp tablet) plus `itemPaddingTop`/`itemPaddingBottom` 8dp→6dp→4dp after the user said it still felt big. Also closed the visible gap between each item's icon and label by overriding Material's internal `mtrl_navigation_bar_item_default_margin` dimen (8dp default → 3dp phone / 4dp tablet) in the app's own `dimens.xml` — `BottomNavigationView` exposes no public XML attribute for that spacing, so shadowing the library's own dimen resource by name (app resources win over the AAR's) was the only way to tighten it.

- **Verification:** `assembleMorphoSmart642Debug` clean after every change (one real error caught and fixed along the way — Android XML comments reject a literal `--`, hit twice while writing explanatory comments). Live-verified on the connected real device (`MPH-MB003A`) after each change via `adb`/screencaps: dead-center taps on Payment now consistently land on the payments list (not the pay flow); `Pay` still opens `GeneratePaymentActivity` correctly; More no longer shows a Payments row; Home/Households/More still navigate correctly with no leftover gap from the removed FAB; nav bar visibly tighter with icon/label close together; Home hero shows only the name line. Did not run the mobile unit test suite (no test changes; scope was layout/navigation/resources only).

### 2026-09-02 — `GeneratePaymentActivity` household picker rebuilt as a bottom sheet with an always-visible search box

- **Starting point:** the "Generated payment" screen's household field was a `TextInputLayout` + `MaterialAutoCompleteTextView` (Material's exposed-dropdown pattern) hinting "Search unpaid household," filtering the default `ArrayAdapter` (word-prefix match only) and showing plain black-on-white `simple_dropdown_item_1line` rows.
- **Iteration 1 — better matching/rows, same widget:** added a custom `HouseholdSearchAdapter` (substring match across name/registration number/village instead of word-prefix-only) and a new `item_household_dropdown.xml` two-line row (bold name + village/reg-number subtitle, app colors) in place of the default Android list item.
- **Iteration 2 — user clarified the actual ask** ("choose unpaid household with the dropdown having the search in it... the dropdown should have a search box... even if there is no household to be paid so the person knows they can search, not just scroll"): the AutoCompleteTextView pattern buries search inside the same field as the trigger and gives no visible affordance when the list is empty. Replaced it with a `BottomSheetDialog` (`bottomsheet_household_picker.xml`) opened by tapping the (now non-editable) field or its new custom chevron-down end icon (`ic_chevron_down.xml`; the field itself became a `TextInputEditText` with `focusable=false`/`inputType=none` so only the sheet's own input takes typing). Inside the sheet: a title, a search box, an `RecyclerView` list (`HouseholdPickerAdapter`, reusing `item_household_dropdown.xml` rows) and an empty-state message — the search box renders unconditionally regardless of how many households are pending. Filtering logic (name/registration-number/village substring match) moved to `filterHouseholds()`/`contains()`, called on every keystroke.
- **Iteration 3 — user follow-up naming/copy fixes:** field hint changed `Search unpaid household` → `Choose unpaid household` → **`Choose household`** (final); removed the `helperText` line under the field entirely ("Only households with a generated, unpaid payment are shown...") per explicit ask rather than trimming it.
- **Iteration 4 — search box itself wasn't fully clickable:** first pass used `androidx.appcompat.widget.SearchView` (the same widget `HouseholdListActivity` already uses) for the sheet's search box; user reported having to tap precisely on the magnifying-glass icon to focus it, and its `queryHint` wasn't rendering as visible placeholder text. Replaced `SearchView` with the same outlined `TextInputLayout`/`TextInputEditText` pattern used everywhere else in this app (new `ic_search.xml` start icon) — because the `EditText` spans the field's full width, a tap anywhere in the box (not just the icon) focuses it, and the hint renders as normal, readable Material placeholder/floating-label text. Filtering switched from `SearchView.OnQueryTextListener` to a plain `TextWatcher`.
- **Verification:** `compileMorphoSmart642DebugJavaWithJavac` clean after every iteration (one real XML error caught: `app:startIconDrawable`/`app:startIconTint` on the sheet's `TextInputLayout` needed `xmlns:app` declared on the layout's root `LinearLayout`, which the file didn't have since it previously only used `android:` attributes). `installMorphoSmart642Debug` + live-verified on the connected real device (`MPH-MB003A`) after each iteration via `adb`/screencaps: field reads "Choose household" with no helper text; tapping it (or the chevron) opens the sheet with the search box visible even at "0 unpaid households"; tapping the sheet's far-right edge (away from the search icon) successfully focuses it and raises the keyboard, confirming the whole-field-clickable fix. **Not verified live end-to-end:** selecting a row and confirming the detail fields (household name/registration number/village/amount) populate correctly — this account's anchor has no payroll/payment cycle generated yet (`Payment pending: 0` on Home, confirmed via `Sync payments` completing with no error and still 0 results, and via backend `SYNC_PAYMENTS` logic requiring a real generated+unpaid row for a household with an enrolled fingerprint/face beneficiary). Verified that specific path by code review instead: `HouseholdPickerAdapter.RowHolder.bind()` and `GeneratePaymentActivity.selectPayment()` both read directly off the same `PaymentDao.LocalPayment` object passed through the row's click listener, so there's no index/lookup step that could desync row-shown-data from fields-populated-data. Attempted to seed a test row directly into the on-device SQLite DB via `adb run-as`/pulled `biopay_agent.db` to force a live check; stopped after the pulled copy came back WAL/journal-inconsistent (`database disk image is malformed`) rather than risk writing back a corrupted file over the device's real app data. A real generated/unpaid payment cycle (via the dashboard's payroll generation, for a household with enrolled biometrics) is needed to close out this last check.

### 2026-09-02 — Home dashboard "Payment status" card: real empty state instead of a stuck-at-0 donut, plus a mislabeled CTA fix

- **Starting point:** the user flagged the "Payment status" card on the mobile Home screen as looking basic/broken — `SimpleDonutChartView` draws only a flat gray track ring and the literal text "0" when its slices sum to zero, which is exactly what every officer sees before any payments exist.
- **Added a real empty state:** new `paymentEmptyState` group in `activity_home.xml` (icon badge, "No payments yet" title, body copy, and an action button) shown whenever `paidCount + pendingPaymentCount == 0`; the existing donut plus a new color-coded legend row (`tvLegendPaid`/`tvLegendPending`, colored dots via `shape_legend_dot.xml`) now live in a `paymentChartGroup` shown only when there's data. `HomeActivity.refreshDashboard()` toggles the two groups' visibility and only touches the chart/legend views when populated. Donut and legend colors changed from `bp_primary`/`bp_secondary` to `bp_success`/`bp_secondary_dark` to match the KPI row's own Paid/Payment-pending accent colors above.
- **User then corrected the CTA's premise:** the first pass labeled the empty-state button "Generate payment" and only then was it pointed out that mobile never generates payments — payroll/payment generation happens on the web dashboard; the mobile officer's only payment action is to *pay* an already-generated, unpaid household. Renamed the button (`btnGeneratePaymentEmpty` → `btnPayEmptyState`) to reuse the exact same label/icon as `PaymentsActivity`'s own `btnPay` (`@string/payment_pay_action` = "Pay", `ic_payments`), and rewrote `home_payment_empty_body` to say payments are generated from the dashboard and to sync to check for new ones. The button still opens `GeneratePaymentActivity`, unchanged — that's the correct destination (same one `PaymentsActivity`'s Pay button uses).
- **Confirmed by code review (no fix needed) that the org-wide scope the user asked for was already correct:** `PaymentDao.countByStatus()` reads the local `payments` table, which `SyncManager.syncPaymentCatalogue()` refreshes from backend `SYNC_PAYMENTS` (`Biometric.java`) — that query has no status filter and scopes only by `organization_code`, so the synced cache already holds every payment (paid and pending) for every household in the org, not just this device's own. The card was empty only because this test org currently has zero generated payments.
- **Verification:** `compileMorphoSmart615DebugJavaWithJavac` clean after the initial pass; `assembleMorphoSmart642Debug` clean after the CTA fix (this account's tablet build is the 642 flavor, not 615 — noted mid-session). Live-verified on the connected real device (`MPH-MB003A`, serial `5041628786`) via `adb install`/screencaps both before and after the CTA fix: empty state renders with icon/title/body/button; tapping **Pay** opens `GeneratePaymentActivity`'s existing "Choose household" picker correctly (its own "0 unpaid households"/"No unpaid household is ready yet" empty state, unchanged). Did not verify the populated donut+legend rendering live — this org has no paid/pending payments yet to trigger that branch; confirmed by reading `SimpleDonutChartView`'s existing slice-drawing logic instead.
- Stopped the standalone backend jar process running locally (`biopay-backend-1.0-SNAPSHOT-jar-with-dependencies.jar`, launched outside Gradle) at the user's request to end that session — the mobile changes above don't depend on the backend running.

### 2026-09-02 - MorphoSmart 6.42 field workflow, sync, locations, household completion, and Home payments workspace

- **Target hardware and shared data model:** confirmed the connected IDEMIA tablet uses the `morphoSmart642` product flavor. Both Morpho variants continue to use the same BioPay backend contracts and organisation-scoped account data; household records are therefore shared through server sync for the same login rather than isolated by APK flavor or device. The real build artifact is under `%LOCALAPPDATA%\BioPay\gradle-build\agent\app\outputs\apk\...`, not the stale repo-local build directory.
- **Sync behavior and feedback:** manual sync actions now start WorkManager work immediately, observe its result, re-enable their controls afterward, and refresh the visible dashboard. Automatic Home refresh uses the automatic-sync path separately. User-facing messages no longer incorrectly claim that every connected-device sync is merely waiting for a network: the app reports connecting/sending, confirms completion, or explains when the tablet has connectivity but the BioPay server did not accept the records. Pending records remain queued safely when the server is unreachable.
- **Interrupt-safe household registration:** a household can be saved and registration finished before fingerprint or face capture. The capture screen explains that the household is already stored locally and offers **Finish and capture later**, preventing loss and a full restart if the tablet powers off. An incomplete household exposes **Capture** and **Add alternate** side by side; a completed household moves **Add alternate** into the former verification-action area because identity verification belongs to the payment workflow. Member cards remain a list of registered people.
- **Location reuse and hierarchy:** manually entered State, County, Location, and Village values are persisted locally and become selectable for later registrations. Dropdown contents are filtered by hierarchy/type, fixing County values such as `Nairobi` or `6000` appearing in the State list. Exposed dropdowns now use the same rounded control radius as other fields. Household summaries/details use State -> County -> Location -> Village, and the obsolete word **Boma** was removed from household-facing copy so `Gataka` is shown as the village rather than "Boma Gataka."
- **Home payment card redesigned after the brainstorming pass:** removed the zero-value donut and its passive status presentation. The lower Home card is now a compact **Payments** workspace. When verifiable pending assignments exist, it shows the assignment count, next household name, registration number/village, amount, a direct **Start payment** action into `PaymentVerificationActivity`, and **View all** for the payment register. With no assignments, it shows **No payment assignments** and a full-width **Check for payments** sync action.
- **Home implementation details:** `HomeActivity.refreshDashboard()` now obtains actionable rows from `PaymentDao.listPendingGeneratedPayments()` rather than deciding the lower-card state from aggregate totals. It retains the operational-summary KPIs, handles a missing household display name safely, formats the amount, and refreshes after manual sync. The old donut/legend and empty-state Pay shortcut were removed from `activity_home.xml`; `bg_badge_neutral.xml` supports the restrained empty state.
- **Quality, build, and install verification:** the Impeccable detector returned `[]`; `git diff --check` reported no whitespace errors (only existing LF-to-CRLF notices); `assembleMorphoSmart642Debug` completed successfully. Installed `app-morphoSmart642-debug.apk` using `adb install -r` on tablet serial `5041628786`, preserving app data, and confirmed it reaches `com.biopay.agent/.home.HomeActivity`. No screenshot was exported because the Home screen can contain sensitive household/payment information.
- **Remaining live check:** the populated next-household card and final payment-verification path require a real dashboard-generated unpaid assignment with an enrolled/verifiable beneficiary. Do not seed or export the tablet SQLite database to manufacture one.

### 2026-09-02 — Dashboard dialog field labels rendering visually cut off

- **Reported symptom:** a screenshot showed the "First name"/"Last name" floating labels in the Officers page's "Register Field Officer" dialog with the tops of the letters sliced off (e.g. the "F" in "First name"). Asked to fix everywhere this pattern appears across the dashboard, not just that one dialog.
- **Root cause:** Vuetify's `.v-label.v-field-label` carries `contain: layout paint`, which — per the CSS containment spec — clips the label's rendered glyphs to its own box exactly like `overflow: hidden` would. That box's height is a line-height computed against this app's `Ubuntu` font (Vuetify's own spacing assumes its default Roboto), and at some fractional device-pixel-ratio/OS display-scaling combinations it rounds a hair short of Ubuntu's actual rendered glyph height, slicing the tops off letters — the same class of sub-pixel rounding bug `ui-fixes.css` already documents fixing for field borders (see that file's existing top comment).
- **Fix:** `frontend/src/styles/ui-fixes.css` — added `.v-label.v-field-label { contain: layout; }`, dropping `paint` from Vuetify's default containment. Paint containment there was only a performance/isolation optimization, not a deliberate clip, so removing it eliminates the clipping risk for every field label app-wide (this class is shared by all `v-text-field`/`v-select`/etc. labels, both floating and static) without any per-page changes.
- **Verification:** could not reproduce the actual clipping in Chrome on this machine at 100%/125%/150% zoom (built a throwaway static repro — `frontend/dev-repro.html` served via a temporary local `python -m http.server`, using the project's real `vue.global.js`/`vuetify.js`/`vuetify.css` UMD builds from `node_modules`, the real Ubuntu Google Font, `html.app-shell-scale` at 15px root, and the exact `OfficersPage.vue` `.field-grid`/compact-density markup); confirmed via JS the label's computed line-height (18px) comfortably exceeds Ubuntu's rendered glyph height in this environment, and confirmed the fix introduces no visual/layout regression at any tested zoom level. Deleted `dev-repro.html` and stopped the temporary test server afterward — nothing left behind. Not verified against the user's actual display-scaling setup that produced the original screenshot; if the clipping recurs, next step is checking the user's Windows display-scaling percentage and OS-level ClearType/font-rendering settings specifically, since a plain browser-zoom test on this machine couldn't reproduce it.

### 2026-09-02 — Mobile: reject fingerprint enrollment when the same finger is already registered to someone else

- **Reported symptom:** the same physical fingerprint could be captured and saved against two different people during household/alternate registration — enrollment never checked a fresh capture against anyone else's already-stored template.
- **Root cause:** `PersonCaptureActivity.captureFingerprint()` saved `device.startCapture()`'s result straight to `FingerprintDao` with no comparison step at all.
- **Fix:** found that the Morpho SDK exposes an offline 1:1 template comparison, `MorphoDevice.verifyMatch(...)`, in both vendor jars (confirmed via `javap` against `MorphoSmart_SDK_6.42.0.0.jar` and `_6.15.3.0.jar`) — unlike `startVerify`, it needs no extra live finger scan, just the device connection already open from the capture. Added `BiometricDevice.templatesMatch(byte[], byte[]) -> MatchResult{MATCHED,NO_MATCH,ERROR}` to the shared interface and implemented it in both `MorphoDeviceAdapter` flavors (642 uses `CustomInteger`/`FalseAcceptanceRate.MORPHO_FAR_5`; 615 has neither, so a raw `far=5` and `Integer.valueOf(0)` are used, matching that flavor's existing `startVerify` convention). Added `FingerprintDao.templatesExcludingBeneficiary()` to pull every other beneficiary's stored template. `PersonCaptureActivity.checkDuplicateThenSave()` now runs after `onCaptured()`, off the main thread: on a match the capture is rejected ("this fingerprint is already registered to another person") and nothing is saved; on a comparison error the save is also blocked, fail-safe, with a "could not verify, try again" message rather than silently allowing an unchecked duplicate; only a clean no-match against everyone else proceeds to save, now confirmed with "Fingerprint captured successfully".
- **Verification:** both flavors compile clean (`compileMorphoSmart642DebugJavaWithJavac`, `compileMorphoSmart615DebugJavaWithJavac`). Not verified against real scanner hardware — the match threshold reuses the existing `FAR_5` rate `startVerify` already used, but this should be confirmed on-device (two genuinely different people's prints should not be flagged as duplicates) before relying on it in the field.

### 2026-09-02 — Dashboard: payment-cycle household step ignored mobile-entered locations; OTP fixes (trim bug + resend)

- **Reported symptom (locations):** on the dashboard's "Generate Payment Cycle" wizard, the Households step's State/County/Location/Village filters didn't show — and couldn't filter by — locations the user had typed manually when registering households on mobile.
- **Root cause:** those dropdowns (and `HouseholdsPage`'s own location filter, which has the identical bug but was out of scope for this pass) are sourced from `GET_STATES`/`GET_COUNTIES`/`GET_LOCATIONS`/`GET_VILLAGES` — the anchor-curated `geo_states`/`geo_counties`/`geo_locations`/`geo_villages` catalogue (`Geography.java`). A household's own `state_code`/`county_code`/`payam_code`/`boma_code` columns are **not** foreign-keyed to that catalogue (`HouseholdFormActivity`'s own comment: "loosely-typed free text columns, no FK to the geo hierarchy") — mobile's manual-entry path stores the officer's typed place name directly in those columns, with no catalogue node ever created for it. So the wizard's dropdowns never listed those names, and selecting a catalogue code filtered against a column that never contained it.
- **Fix:** added `GET_HOUSEHOLD_LOCATIONS { level: STATE|COUNTY|LOCATION|VILLAGE }` to `Household.java` — lists the *distinct values actually present on households in scope* (`state_code`/`county_code`/`payam_code`/`boma_code`), `LEFT JOIN`ing the matching `geo_*` table to resolve a friendly name when the stored value happens to be an official code, falling back to the raw stored value otherwise (which for a manual entry already **is** the place name). Registered its permission scope in `PermissionPolicy.java` (same three permissions `GET_HOUSEHOLDS` accepts) and its processing code in `frontend/src/api/processingCodes.ts`. `PayrollGeneratePage.vue`'s `loadGeoLookups()` now calls this instead of the geography endpoints, scoped by the chosen organisation, and re-fetches (clearing any stale filter) whenever the organisation changes.
- **Reported symptom (OTP):** entering the emailed verification code on the same wizard's final step and clicking "Confirm & Generate" reported the code as invalid.
- **Root cause:** `Payroll.java`'s `generate()` read `otpCode` from the payload without trimming it (`payload.getString("otpCode", "")`), while the sibling `approve()` handler already trims (`.trim()`). A copy-pasted code carrying a trailing space/newline — a common artifact from some email clients — hashes to a different value than the stored hash, so `otpService.verify()` legitimately (but confusingly) reports "Invalid or expired verification code" for an otherwise-correct code.
- **Fix:** added the same `.trim()` in `Payroll.java`'s `generate()`, and trimmed `genForm.value.otpCode` again on the frontend before dispatching `GENERATE_PAYROLL`, belt-and-suspenders.
- **Also added (explicitly requested):** a way to request a new code — previously the "Send Verification Code" button vanished for good once clicked, so an expired (5-minute TTL) or mistyped code left no recovery but restarting the whole 4-step wizard. Added a "Resend code" text button next to the code field once a code has been sent; it re-issues via the same `REQUEST_PAYROLL_OTP` call and clears the stale input, and needed no backend change since `OtpService.verify()` already always checks the most-recently-issued code for that user/reference type.
- **Verification:** `mvn -q compile` (backend) and `npm run build` (`vue-tsc -b && vite build`, frontend) both clean. Not live-verified end-to-end against a real mailbox/SMTP send in this session — the trim fix and the new distinct-locations query were verified by code reading and by the two clean builds, not by walking the full wizard with a real household that has a manually-typed mobile location.

Confirmed the app launches (to `LoginActivity`) with no crash via `adb shell monkey -c android.intent.category.LAUNCHER` + `dumpsys activity activities`.
